# The journey as a text adventure (learner child)

Everything the game said and everything the child did, level by level, in play order. Times are game seconds.

## Ninja Training (tutorial)

   0.0s   --- tut-gong ---
   0.1s   > child taps: gong
   1.7s   [gong]
   1.7s SENSEI: Great tapping!
   2.5s   > child taps: gong
   2.9s SENSEI: Whenever you're stuck, tap me! I'm always here in the corner, and I'll tell you what to do. Try it now. Tap me!
   3.7s   --- tut-help ---
   3.7s   [great]
   3.7s   > child taps: Help
   3.8s SENSEI: That's it! And if you're still stuck, tap me again, and again, and I'll give you bigger clues.
   6.2s   > child taps: Help
   8.6s   > child taps: Help
   9.8s SENSEI: Tap the...
   9.8s   --- find ---
   9.8s   [good]
   9.8s   > child taps: sun
      🔊 "sun"
  10.5s SENSEI: Brilliant!
  11.6s SENSEI: Tap the speaker to hear the sound again.
  12.2s   --- tut-speaker ---
  12.2s   > child taps: Hear it again
  12.2s SENSEI: Tap the...
  13.4s SENSEI: Tap the...
  14.7s   --- find ---
  14.7s   [good]
  14.7s   > child taps: mat
      🔊 "mat"
  15.4s SENSEI: Brilliant!
  16.9s SENSEI: Ninja power!
  17.1s   > child taps: mat
  18.1s SENSEI: Now it's your turn!
  18.3s   > child taps: fan
  18.3s   [good]
      🔊 "fan"
  19.1s SENSEI: Brilliant!
  20.2s   [fanfare]
  20.3s SENSEI: You're ready, ninja! Let's go!
  21.1s   [great]
  22.5s SENSEI: Welcome to Bamboo Village!
  24.6s SENSEI: Tap the glowing stone to start your next adventure.

## Show Sensei (placement)

   0.0s SENSEI: Do you know some sounds already? Tap the little seed if you're just starting. Tap the star to show me what you know!
   0.1s   --- place-ask ---
   0.1s   > child taps: Show Sensei what I know
   1.4s   [great]
   1.4s SENSEI: Let's see what you know! Just have a go.
   2.6s   --- find ---
   2.6s   [good]
   2.6s   > child taps: a
   3.4s SENSEI: Which is the spelling of this sound?
   3.8s   [good]
   3.8s   > child taps: n
   4.7s SENSEI: Brilliant!
   5.1s   > child taps: i
   5.8s SENSEI: Which one is...
      🔊 "pot"
   8.7s   [good]
   8.7s   > child taps: pot
   9.0s SENSEI: Ninja power!
  11.1s   > child taps: pot
  12.3s   > child taps: pit
  13.4s SENSEI: Which one is...
      🔊 "tap"
  16.0s   [good]
  16.0s   > child taps: tap
  17.0s SENSEI: Brilliant!
  18.1s SENSEI: Which one is...
  18.4s   [good]
  18.4s   > child taps: cob
  19.3s SENSEI: Which one is...
  19.6s   [wrong]
  19.6s   > child taps: fit
  20.3s   [fanfare]
  20.4s SENSEI: Wow! Now I know just where to start your adventure. Let's go!
  21.3s   [great]
  24.8s SENSEI: Welcome to Blossom Hills!
  26.7s SENSEI: Tap the glowing stone to start your next adventure.
